# **Motorhome Hire** from twoof theWorld’s Largest Rental Companies

Just go Hertz Motorhome hire is an exclusive partnership between **Hertz** and **Just go** motorhomes. With over **a century of experience** in vehicle rentals, Hertz have partnered with Just go, the UK’s **premier motorhome hire company**, to offer customers **a new way to travel**.

## **Great rental prices** on automatic motorhomes with unlimited mileage

Just go Hertz offer a [luxury range of rental motorhomes](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/motorhomes.html) that are available to [hire from any of our UK wide depots](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/location.html). Whether you are planning a **family holiday**, travelling as a **couple** or with a **group of friends**, there is a Just go Hertz [motorhome model](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/motorhomes.html) for everyone. Create your own flexible itinerary or get inspiration from our team.

Find the Just go motorhome that’s right for you.

[Get started on your break](https://bookingprod.justgo.uk.com/?open-mobile=true&ac=HERTZ)

### Luxury & Modern Motorhomes

Experience the ultimate in travel comfort and style with our selection of luxurious and modern motorhomes.

### Convenient UK & Ireland Wide Locations

Explore the UK and Ireland with ease, thanks to our wide range of conveniently located pick-up and drop-off points.

### All Campers, Motorhomes & RVs Under 2 Years Old

Enjoy a modern adventure with our fleet of brand-new campers, motorhomes, and RVs, all under two years old.

### Easy Drive, Automatic Gearboxes

Drive with confidence and ease, thanks to our automatic gearboxes and easy-to-use controls.

### Unlimited Mileage on RV Rentals

Explore the UK and Ireland with unlimited mileage, so you can travel as far as you want without worrying about mileage limits.

This 4 berth is perfect for families as it offers twin rear beds in a separate room.

Perfect for families and friends

### Shower / Toilet

Hot showers & on-board toilet

With an oven, grill and all essentials

That’s under 24 months old

## Frequently Asked Questions

Find answers to commonly asked questions about motorhome hire

How much does it cost to hire a motorhome?

The cost of hiring a motorhome depends on the size of the vehicle and the time of year. For small and medium-sized motorhomes, you can expect to pay between £600 and £900 per week. Larger motorhomes with more features and space can cost up to £1500 for a weeklong hire. These prices are based on average rates. It's important to note that the most popular times for campervan hire are typically late spring, summer, and early autumn. During these times, you will find that prices are higher, and availability is limited. If you're looking to secure the best deal, it's a good idea to book your motorhome hire well in advance. Additionally, consider hiring outside of the peak season for lower prices and more availability.

Who are the best motorhome hire company?

Several reputable companies offer motorhome and campervan hire in the UK, each with its own strengths. Just go, Bunk Campers, Apollo and Hertz are popular choices with large fleets and availability. These all have locations across the UK and Ireland, giving access to Europe too.

Can you drive a motorhome on a standard UK driving licence?

Yes, you can drive a motorhome on a standard UK car driving license (Category B) if the motorhome’s Maximum Authorised Mass (MAM) is 3,500kg or less. All motorhomes on our fleet are under 3,500kg and can therefore be driven on a standard UK car driving license or equivalent.

What’s the difference between RV, Campervan and Motorhome hire?

In summary, campervans, RVs and motorhomes are self-drive vehicles designed for recreational travel and living, but they differ primarily in size and features. All three are used to describe motorhomes in the UK and Ireland, however the word campervan sometimes refers to the smaller, more compact VW. RVs (Recreational Vehicles) is a broader term, originally American that’s used to describe both motorhomes and campervans, but sometimes refers to larger, more luxurious vehicles with extensive amenities. Motorhomes are generally interpreted as being larger than campervans, offering more space and features like bathrooms, separate living and sleeping areas, with more extensive kitchens.

## Motorhomes grow in popularity

According to Bunk Campers, Industry figures show new motorhome registrations in the UK rose from 8487 in 2004 to 16447 in 2024 a 93.7% increase. [(4)](https://bunkcampers.com/#citation-1 "Bunk Campers - Motorhomes grow in popularity")

## Growing Popularity

Motorhome hire has seen a significant increase in popularity in recent years. According to a report by the UK's Camping and Caravanning Club, motorhome holidays have become increasingly popular with a 20% increase in Brits considering a touring holiday. Ownership is expected to grown by 40% by 2030. [(1)](https://www.caravanclub.co.uk/media-centre/press-releases/touring-holidays-surge-in-popularity-as-many-look-for-affordable-holiday-options/ "Camping and Caravanning Club - Touring Holidays Surge in Popularity as Many Look for Affordable Holiday Options")

## Environmental benefits

Motorhomes can be an eco-friendly way to travel compared to traditional holidays. A 500 mile road trip creates 20% of the carbon compared to a one-way flight from London to Tenerife (This has been calculated using carbon-clear.com and resurgence.org). Or if comparing to return flights it's just 10%. [(2)](https://bunkcampers.com/campervan-hire-and-sustainable-tourism/ "Bunk Campers - Campervan Hire and Sustainable Tourism")

## Economic impact

The broader Holiday Park and Campsite sector contributes significantly to the UK economy. According to a report by the UK Caravan & Camping Alliance (UKCCA), the industry generated £12.2 billion for the UK economy in visitor spending during 2024. [(3)](https://www.ukcca.org.uk/media/twjpssgp/ukcca-economic-impact-report-2024.pdf "UK Caravan Council - The Economic Impact of Caravanning and Motorhoming in the UK")

# About Us

Hertz Motorhome hire is an exclusive partnership between Just go motorhomes and Hertz. With over 90 years of experience in car and van rentals, Hertz have teamed up with Just go (the UKs premier motorhome hire company) to offer their customers a different way to travel. Don’t worry if you’ve never hired a motorhome before, Just go’s expert team will give you a full tour to show you how to operate the vehicle and to answer any questions you may have. Once you’ve hit the road, the on-road support team are available by telephone if you need any additional assistance. Read More

Whether you are planning a family holiday, travelling as a couple or with a group of friends, there is a motorhome model for everyone. Enjoy the convenience of having a flexible itinerary and fulfilling your travel needs without changing hotels or staying in a cold tent!

Just imagine waking up in a luxurious motorhome, taking a nice warm shower, putting the kettle on and stepping outside to a beautiful beach in Devon; a ski run in Austria; a vineyard in France; or perhaps your favourite distillery in Scotland. The possibilities are endless; you can just go anywhere!

## What’s Included?

All Just go motorhomes are fully equipped so that you experience all the comforts from home in a luxurious yet comfortable environment and they even include hot running water and heating. A shower and toilet are standard on all models, offering maximum convenience for travellers not having to be reliant on on-site facilities. All motorhomes have kitchens furnished with a grill or oven (dependant on motorhome model) a fridge, kettle, toaster, crockery, cutlery, pots, pans, cooking utensils and plastic tumblers. We supply all the tools of the trade so you can enjoy cooking your evening meal! Read More

For travelling, each motorhome comes with comprehensive UK insurance for one driver, unlimited mileage, and automatic transmission—making your journey smooth and worry-free. You’ll also have access to Sat Nav, Android Auto, or Apple CarPlay\* to help guide you to some of the most unique and breath-taking destinations. On the exterior, you’ll find a reversing camera and a solar panel, adding convenience and sustainability to your adventure.

New to driving a motorhome? We offer free roadside assistance and there is also a European travel pack (beam benders, bulb and fuse packs) included as well.

If a motorhome holiday is only part of your travelling experience and you do not want to travel with your luggage after unpacking into the motorhome, you can leave your suitcases with us in storage during your hire.

## Description

**The Adventurer 6 berth motorhome has versatile sleeping arrangements with bunk beds and two doubles.**

Perfect for families and groups heading to festivals or campsites this model is the only Hertz motorhome that includes bunk beds. The tall fridge with freezer compartment and gas oven/grill in the well-equipped kitchen area a make cooking a dream. Opposite you will find the roomy shower room which separates the bunk beds from the other sleeping areas, ideal for younger members of your party.

Additionally the large storage compartment at the back of the vehicle is ideal for skis, surfboards, bikes, chairs and other outdoor gear with the lower bunk able to be raised to create extra storage.

This motorhome is designed for comfort and practicality which makes it one of our most popular rentals. The layout means that everyone has a cosy spot to sleep while ample storage keeps your journey clutter free.

- Auto Stop Start Technology

# Motorhome Hire for **Northern Ireland Adventures**

Discovering Northern Ireland by hiring a motorhome offers travellers an exceptional way to explore the country’s beauty. At Just go Hertz we offer an unlimited mileage policy meaning you can travel without restriction across a vast distance – a freedom not often afforded by other travel options.

Take in stunning coastal landscapes and scenery by strolling along the quiet country roads, stopping where you choose for the night. You will be greeted by our friendly team of staff at our Belfast rental depot; they will be happy to assist you with planning an itinerary and suggesting routes that cover Northern Ireland and Ireland.

All of our motorhomes are modern and comfortable in their style with each of them also being automatic and easy to manoeuvre. With hot showers on board, you will feel like you are travelling home from home. Embark on your Northern Ireland motorhome adventure today.

## **How Your Belfast Motorhome Hire Works**

1. **Your adventure begins** as soon as you arrive at our friendly Belfast rental depot. One of our motorhome specialists will greet you and explain your motorhome hire in detail. They will talk you through what to expect and give you some advice on your journey ahead.
2. **Once you hit the open roads** of Northern Ireland the choice is yours on where your adventure takes you. Our useful guide may help you design and plan a trip that is personalised to you and your fellow travellers.
3. **Bringing your motorhome back** to Belfast is simple and straightforward. We will check the vehicle over so you can get back on the road to home. If you need onward transport to the close by airport we are more than happy to arrange that for you.

# Contact Us

Just go has depots located in Toddington - Bedfordshire, Edinburgh - Scotland, Dublin - Ireland and Belfast - Northern Ireland.

To quickly contact the UK office call [+44 (0) 1525 878000](tel:+441525878000).

**Greater London -** Just go, Redhill Farm, Harlington Road, Toddington, Bedfordshire, LU5 6HF.

**Edinburgh Airport -** Just go, 2 Drovers Road, East Mains Industrial Estate, Broxburn, West Lothian, EH52 5ND.

**Dublin, Ireland -** Clonswords, Ballyboughal, Co. Dublin, A41 F308, Ireland

**Belfast, Northern Ireland -** Cyril Johnston Complex, Ballynahinch Road, Carryduff, BT8 8DJ, Northern Ireland.

# Motorhome Hire for **Ireland Adventures**

Begin your Irish journey by picking up your motorhome rental in the vibrant capital, Dublin. Ireland has it all: cultural cities, rolling countryside, national parks and historic world heritage sites. What better way to visit everything in one single trip than by motorhome? You won’t be bound by timelines or miles, you will have the freedom to choose your own destinations and journey.

When you hire one of our carefully selected motorhomes, you will find a modern vehicle with an automatic transmission and plenty of space to holiday as you wish. Newer motorhomes are packed with features, which means you can be self-sufficient and even head off-grid, thanks to solar panels and on-board facilities. Many of our models have multi-berth occupancy suitable for larger groups with flexible yet comfortable beds to ensure you get a great night’s sleep, no matter your Irish destination.

Whether you are new to touring or have past experience, we are here to help answer your questions and get you on the road. ## **How Your Dublin Motorhome Hire Works**

1. **Collect your motorhome from our Hertz Dublin depot.** Our friendly and knowledgeable team will walk you through everything you need to know, from driving tips to campsite setup, so you can hit the road with confidence and start your Irish adventure.
2. **Plan out your trip in advance, or see where the wind takes you.** From the streets of Galway to the Cliffs of Moher, or spots off the beaten track, the choice is entirely yours. Motorhomes enable a trip with multiple stops easily, but can also suit those looking for a one stop journey for full relaxation.
3. **When your hire period comes to an end.** Simply make your way back to Dublin, where we will check in your vehicle and see you home with help on your final travel arrangements if you so wish.

# Motorhome Hire for **Travel Adventures in Scotland**

Scotland has to be the ultimate destination to explore by motorhome. The roads and campsites have the most amazing scenery that you would otherwise miss with other, more standard holidays. Our Edinburgh hire depot is the perfect spot to collect your motorhome, with easy access by plane, train, bus or car, so that you can head off on your Scotland adventure right away.

The dramatic mountains, valleys, lochs and beaches of the country truly are a sight to behold. Whether you are planning to travel the **North Coast 500, a visit to iconic Harry Potter filming locations or drive the South West Coastal 300**, our reliable motorhome hire can take you to every corner of Scotland thanks to our unlimited mileage.

Our motorhomes give you a slice of luxury on the road, with **room to relax, cook, dine, entertain and get a good night’s sleep** after a day of sightseeing. Stock your fridge with local ingredients and prepare your meals with a view before settling down for the evening. With an on board hot shower and large, sumptuous beds, you will come back rested and refreshed as you drop your motorhome back to us in Edinburgh.

[Arrange A Free Quote](https://bookingprod.justgo.uk.com/?open-mobile=true&ac=HERTZ)

## **What Happens When You Book** Your Motorhome Hire With Us?

A trip to Scotland calls for a flexible method of transport, which is where a motorhome comes in. **Easy to drive, set up and pack away**, you can plan a multi-destination trip with complete ease. Give yourself time in between sites to soak up the local culture, the historic sites and the beauty of the landscape. Whether you are looking to climb the peaks, hike trails, immerse yourself in local towns and villages or visit the cities, Scotland has an array of attractions to enjoy. The perk of having no cap on the miles you can travel enables you to really go the distance to explore this magical country. Read More

Your holiday starts when you arrive at the Hertz Edinburgh motorhome hire depot, where our motorhome experts await your arrival. You will be greeted and shown to your vehicle, where you’ll be shown **how to work everything** down to connecting your electric cables. They can also give tips on driving and some of the best spots to stay.

After you drive away, the open road is ready and waiting for you. You get the freedom to pick your own route and itinerary, which makes motorhoming one of the most incredible holiday experiences. If you’d like some help on where to visit, our **area guides** are a great place to begin.

Get ready for the adventure of a lifetime around Scotland, from Hadrian’s Wall to the Outer Hebrides (accessible by ferry), there is so much to discover. Whether you are travelling alone or with a group of friends or family, our motorhomes will take you to **every leg of your journey in comfort**. Once you arrive back at the Edinburgh depot we’ll have you checked in in no time. Plus if you’d like some extra help organising taxis, airport transfers or other travel tickets, we can arrange this for you.

Here at Hertz, you’ll find a choice of 4 berth motorhomes, each packed full with features and specifications designed to make your journey stylish, practical and relaxing. Whether you're a couple seeking peace and quiet or a family of four wanting to explore with children, we have the ideal motorhome tailored to your needs. Read More

Our four berth motorhomes offer different layouts to suit your group. The vehicles offer spacious living areas including dinettes with comfy seating for mealtimes and space to kickback and unwind in the evenings. Driving is made enjoyable with Bluetooth and radio fitted as standard across our fleet so you can pop your favourite playlist on whilst navigating the roads.

# **Spacious Living & Comfortable Beds** In Our 5 Berth Motorhomes

Are you looking for a motorhome that has space and comfort? Take to the road with one of our five berth motorhomes which features a flexible layout. 5 berth models are perfect to accommodate groups of friends or families. You can expect generous living areas and adaptable sleeping arrangements that transform simply from daytime seating to cosy sleeping nooks.

The Navigator model, for example, is designed with flexibility in mind. The rear lounge transforms effortlessly into either two single beds or one large double, giving you options for how you sleep and travel. At the front, an electric drop-down bed adds extra space, and the dinette area converts into another double. The unique flexibility of the Navigator makes it ideal for longer trips with more travellers.Read More

Every five berth motorhome we have for hire is equipped with the essential amenities for your break. A shower and separate bathroom, fridge, oven and hob all come as standard. Then there are the roomy seating zones that are perfect for dining or unwinding after a day of exploration.

Booking your motorhome hire with Hertz doesn’t end at pickup. Our dedicated team give expert phone and email support throughout your journey. With years of experience we know all the little details of our vehicles. From guidance on setup or troubleshooting we’re on hand to help so that your trip is truly memorable.

# **Enjoy Exciting Group Adventures** with our 6 Berth Motorhome Fleet

Here at Hertz we have a range of 6 berth motorhomes for hire which are ideal for group holidays with family and friends. Explore the UK in comfort with our fleet designed for space and convenience. Whether you’re planning a slow scenic route or a short city break, our six berth motorhomes are modern and stylish which make the perfect base to create special memories together.

Our motorhomes come equipped with the latest features to really enhance your journey. On collection you can expect to find a reversing camera, kitchen essentials, solar panels and electric hook up cables. If you would like an extra touch of luxury we have optional upgrades such as linen or bike racks.Read More

Booking your motorhome trip with Hertz means you’re never alone on the road. Our experienced team gives phone and email support on any questions or issues. With years of knowledge we know everything about our motorhomes so that your trip runs smoothly. After all, we want you to come away with wonderful memories of a very special break.

# Motorhome Hire for **Travel Adventures in the UK**

Exploring all the UK has to offer in a Hertz motorhome is the perfect way to travel in style and comfort. Collecting your motorhome from our London depot will give you the opportunity to **visit the diverse landscapes across England, Wales and even Scotland** if you fancy a proper road trip. You will be spoilt for choice thanks to the UK’s historic cities and buildings with sightseeing spots, landmarks and tourist favourites you won’t want to miss. Then there is the gorgeous rolling countryside, lakes, mountains, heathlands, woodland and coastlines that will give you **views and surroundings that only a motorhome stay can offer**.

Our motorhomes are **easy to drive and operate**, being modern and automatic, they are also packed with the latest equipment and technology. Parking will be a breeze with many models featuring rear cameras. When pitching up you can truly relax thanks to the on board hot showers, comfortable beds, fully equipped kitchenettes and cosy seating areas. Your motorhome will become **your home on wheels** as you visit some of the most beautiful parts of the country.

## **You Can Count on Hertz** for Your Motorhome Hire

Renting your motorhome from Hertz is the foundation of a memorable trip across the UK. From the winding lanes of Cornwall to the history hidden in the walls of stately homes and medieval castles, **your motorhome will offer access to a vast range of locations**. Travelling by motorhome gives flexibility not available with other touring types, they are **easy to pitch up and pack down**, which makes a multi destination trip easy. Imagine waking up with a different view each day, ready to find a new hidden gem and our unlimited mileage will give you the freedom to travel far and wide. Read More

Pick up from our Greater London depot, situated at our Toddington HQ, couldn’t be simpler. When you arrive one of our welcoming team members will be there to greet you and take you to your vehicle. You will be **shown around the motorhome and how it works** before you set off.

Once you drive away, your holiday is entirely in your hands. Map out your bespoke route, itinerary and timings so that **your trip is tailored exactly to your wishes**. Customers find our area guides helpful for holiday planning which feature some of the best spots in the UK to visit.

Whether you are journeying solo, as a couple, with family or friends, our motorhomes are **perfectly set up** to accompany you on your UK adventure. From the views of the Peak District to the cobbled streets of York, the vibrant Brighton pier or the iconic Cotswolds region, you will be sure to have a wonderful holiday.

At the end of your journey, our Greater London depot team will be ready to check in your motorhome. But we don’t end our service there, if you’d like help with London airport transfers, taxis or other public transport booking, just ask.

## Description

### Discover Budget-Friendly Motorhome Hire with Mystery Motorhomes

Make the most of up to 20% savings when you choose our Mystery 4 motorhome hire. By taking a chance on our Mystery 4 choice of motorhome you will receive a hand chosen motorhome from our wide ranging fleet of Hertz, Just go, Bunk Campers or Apollo motorhomes.

Every motorhome is equipped with all you need to guarantee a smooth running holiday that features your chosen berth size including kitchenettes kitted out with all necessary accessories, bathrooms and lounges for comfort and social space. By opting for our Mystery motorhome the details of your vehicle will remain a surprise until your trip starts!

Designed with couples or solo travellers in mind, the Mystery 4 is a great choice for people looking to embrace any motorhome they are allocated. You won’t know which model you’ll get until collection day, which all adds to the element of adventure in the build up to your holiday preparation!

If you aren’t looking for an element of surprise and would prefer to select a specific layout or vehicle, feel free to browse our entire motorhome collection.

## Description

**Our Navigator 5 berth motorhome has sleeping arrangements for 5 people and a U shape lounge layout, spacious enough for socialising.**

The motorhome’s design allows the space to feel open and inviting for group travel whilst providing cosy sleeping areas when needed, accommodating up to five guests with ease. The rear U shape sofa simply converts into a large double bed for a restful night’s sleep. An electric drop-down bed at the front of the motorhome and the dinette area which converts into a double are also available for sleeping options, making it ideal for families or groups of friends.

The Navigator’s clever layout features a central kitchen, wardrobe and shower room, with the two driver’s seats able to swivel around for additional seating during your site visits. The solar panel is ideal for keeping you powered on off grid adventures while the gas heating will keep you warm on cooler journeys.

[Request a personalised walkthrough](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/contact.html)

Our Flexible Change Cover offers guests the flexibility to amend or cancel their booking without fees.Read More

Our Flexible Change Cover offers guests the flexibility to amend or cancel their booking without fees.

- Cancel free of charge up to 14 nights before the pick up date.

- Drivers Cab Arm Rests
- Auto Stop Start Technology
- 65 litres fuel capacity
- Length: 7.24m
- Width (mirrors folded): 2.35m
- Height: 2.95m
- Interior Height: 2.08m

## Description

**The 4-berth Pathfinder motorhome suits families brilliantly, boasting private space at the back of the motorhome with twin beds.**

Perfect for families or friends, the Pathfinder motorhome has two single beds separated by a dividing door at the rear. A double bed neatly folds down from the ceiling above the dinette, adding extra sleeping space without making the living area too busy. The motorhome is fully equipped to maintain an impressive level of comfort allowing you to be self-sufficient whilst on your travels. It includes a 100 watt solar panel, a full-colour reversing camera, and a practical L-shaped kitchen with oven and a fridge that has a freezer compartment.

[Request a personalised walkthrough](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/contact.html)

## Description

**The Rainbow is Hertz's top-selling motorhome, featuring a low-profile design with a double bed which is positioned either as a drop-down bed above the dining area or fixed at the rear of the vehicle.**

This popular model comes equipped with all the necessary essentials for couples to enjoy a hassle-free break including a modern shower and toilet. Cooking facilities and food storage options along with a comfortable dinette, ensure every trip is both practical and relaxing.

Explore the Hertz Rainbow motorhome today and experience the perfect mix of space and comfort.

[Request a personalised walkthrough](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/contact.html)

### Flexible Change Cover

Our Flexible Change Cover offers guests the flexibility to amend or cancel their booking without fees.Read More

Our Flexible Change Cover offers guests the flexibility to amend or cancel their booking without fees.

- Cancel free of charge up to 14 nights before the pick up date.
- One free booking amendment (subject to availability).
  - Flexible Change Cover must be added at the time of booking
  - The guest is liable to pay any price increase on the booking due to the amendment

**£50** per hire

## Vehicle Specifications

- 2024 or newer
- Make: Fiat or Ford
- 4 seatbelts
- Automatic transmission
- 2x4 front wheel drive
- Diesel
- Power steering & cruise control
- Reversing camera
- Bike rack as optional extra
- Driver's Cab Air Conditioning
- Driver's Cab DAB Radio / Bluetooth / USB
- Sat Nav / Android Auto / Apple CarPlay
- Electric Wing Mirrors
- Drivers Cab Arm Rests
- Auto Stop Start Technology
- 70 litres fuel capacity
- Length: 5.99-7.40m
- Width (mirrors folded): 2.14 – 2.35m
- Height: 2.95m
- Interior Height: 1.95m

## Cooking Facilities

- 2 burner gas hob
- Gas oven
- Sink
- Fridge
- Freezer compartment

## Living Amenities

- Internal Walk-Through
- Dual Battery System (Leisure/Vehicle)
- Shower
- Toilet
- Gas Heating
- Hot/Cold Water
- 12v/230v Power Supply
- 230v Sockets
- 12v Sockets
- USB Sockets
- Solar Panel
- Fresh water tank 100l
- Waste (grey) water tank 80l

## Sleeping Arrangements

- Sleeps 2
- Double bed (layout varies) - 1.90m x 1.32m

## Description

**The Voyager easily accommodates four people with its thoughtful design. At the back, a door takes you to a private bedroom with an island bed flanked by bedside tables, creating an en-suite space.**

Ideal for couples seeking distinct living and sleeping spaces, The Voyager boasts a spacious living area. The lounge comes with swivel chairs, while a second double bed drops down electronically from the ceiling above the dining area.

- 2024 or newer
- Make: Fiat or Ford
- 4 seatbelts
- 2 Isofix
- Automatic transmission
- 2x4 front wheel drive
- Power steering & cruise control
- Reversing camera
- Bike rack as optional extra
- Driver's Cab Air Conditioning
- Driver's Cab DAB Radio / Bluetooth / USB
- Sat Nav / Android Auto / Apple CarPlay
- Electric Wing Mirrors
- Drivers Cab Arm Rests
- Auto Stop Start Technology
- 65 litres fuel capacity
- Length: 7.45m
- Width (mirrors folded): 2.35m
- Height: 2.95m
- Interior Height: 2.08m

Just go Hertz proudly serves as the official UK motorhome rental partner of the Caravan and Motorhome Club, the top-rated touring club in the UK. As part of our rental partnership, all Just go Hertz guests can access the Caravan and Motorhome Club’s Campsite+ Scheme. Read More

Campsite+ unlocks a world of camping benefits. With over 2,700 motorhome sites in the UK and Europe, our guests can gain entry to Club campsites at exclusive member rates, saving you £15 per night at any of the quality UK Club campsites.

The Caravan and Motorhome Club campsites offer top-class facilities including complimentary hot showers, electric hook-up facilities, Wi-Fi, and even the perk of having your furry friends and little ones under 5 stay for free at certain campsites.

European campsite discounts

If you are planning an unforgettable motorhome holiday touring Europe, then rest assured you can still access campsite discounts in Europe with Campsite+. With over 300 European campsites in 15 different countries across the continent, you can find a campsite to suit your holiday. Discover the beautiful landscapes and vibrant cultures across Europe. Research the European campsites available [here](https://www.caravanclub.co.uk/overseas-holidays/overseas-sites/).

How to access discounts

Browse a wide range of campsites on the Caravan and Motorhome Club’s website and find the perfect campsite for your next UK motorhome holiday. After booking your Just go Hertz motorhome holiday, you will receive details on how to access Campsite+ discounts in your booking confirmation email.

Follow the instructions in your email to receive your unique access code. Once you receive your access code, you will be able to book campsites directly on the Caravan and Motorhome Club website.

T&C’s. At all UK Club campsites: Under 5’s and dogs stay FREE. Awnings are included at no extra cost when staying on an appropriate pitch type. FREE electric hook-up when staying on an appropriate pitch type (excludes Wyatts Covert Club Campsite and Non-electric Economy pitches). Hot showers are FREE on appropriate campsites with shower facilities. FREE Wi-Fi service available at 146 UK Club campsites.

## Motorhome Campsite **FAQs**

Do I need to make a reservation for a campsite?

Yes. We recommend booking your campsites in advance, especially during the peak summer months. Popular campsites can fill up in the summer so early booking is advised. Many campsites allow you to book online, over the phone or by email.

What information do I need to make a reservation?

Typically you will need the dates of your stay, number of people staying, length of your motorhome (which can be found on the Just go Hertz website), and any specific requirements.

How often should I stay at campsites?

We recommend staying at campsites every night if possible. At a minimum, you should stay at campsites every 1-2 nights to recharge your motorhome leisure battery. Campsites also have great facilities to support your motorhome trip including showers, toilets, waste emptying stations for grey waste water and toilet cassettes as well as fresh water fill-up points.

Are campsites pet-friendly?

Many campsites across the UK & Ireland are pet-friendly, however, some have restrictions on the number of pets and some do not welcome pets at all. It is important to check with the campsite in advance if you intend to stay with your pet.

How do I dispose of waste at a campsite?

The campsite will have facilities so you can properly dispose of your waste, grey waste water, and toilet cassettes. Please check with the campsite host on arrival and follow their instructions to dispose of your waste. Please remember to follow the Leave No Trace Principle and leave the land / area the same as when you found it.

## Dog-Friendly Motorhome Hire

# **Dog-Friendly** Motorhome Hire

Your dog deserves a motorhome holiday too, and there’s no better way to experience the beauty of the UK & Ireland than with your four-legged friend by your side. All Just go Hertz motorhomes are dog-friendly and you can travel with up to 2 dogs per motorhome for a small fee of £50/€60.

We make it easy for your canine companion to join you on your adventure.

[Book Now](https://bookingprod.justgo.uk.com/?open-mobile=true&ac=HERTZ)

![Dog-Friendly Motorhome Hire](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/img/dog-friendly-1.jpg?v=2.3)

## Top Motorhome **Travel Tips** For Travelling With Your Dog

To help you plan and make the most of your motorhome holiday with your four-legged friend, view our helpful tips and advice.

[Dogs & Campsites](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/promotions/dog-friendly-motorhomes.html#campsites) [Dogs & Europe Motorhome Holidays](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/promotions/dog-friendly-motorhomes.html#europe)

## Top Motorhome **Travel Tips** for Travelling With Your Dog

1. Bring your pet a water bowl, a food bowl and their favourite toy for the adventure.
2. Encourage your dog to feel safe. When you first get on-board, let your dog sniff around and settle into their new home on wheels.
3. Schedule breaks from driving to allow your dog time to go to the toilet and walk around.
4. Don’t forget their favourite food and snack of choice—even if it is a bone to chew on.
5. If they find a waterlogged stick, let them bring it home to the motorhome. In a dog's life, it’s the simple pleasures that make the big difference.
6. Create a comfortable sleeping environment with their bed from home if possible.
7. Before booking the motorhome holiday, ask yourself, will my dog enjoy long drives? Some dogs can find long drives uncomfortable. Ensure your dog can tolerate driving before you get started on your driving adventure.
8. We recommend keeping the interior of the motorhome clean and tidy. If possible, cover the bed and living area.

## Dogs & **Campsites**

Not every campsite will allow dogs. Always check with the campsite before and see if they allow dogs, how many dogs per guest, and what their rules are. You will find hundreds of dog-friendly campsites in England, Wales, Scotland and Northern Ireland. You can use helpful sites like Pitchup.com to filter by dog-friendly campsites using the ‘dogs allowed’ filter.

Some campsites ask that dogs are always on a lead whilst on-site. Before setting off on your motorhome holiday ensure your dog’s collar and harness fit properly and still give them enough room to explore their surroundings and be comfortable.

Through our exclusive membership with the Camping and Caravanning Club, you can get discounts on over 1000 campsites that offer dog-friendly camping for no extra fee. To find dog-friendly campsites head over to the [Camping and Caravanning Club](https://www.campingandcaravanningclub.co.uk/campsites/dog-friendly/).

![Dog-Friendly Motorhome Hire](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/img/dog-friendly-2.jpg?v=2.3)

## Dogs & **Europe** Motorhome Holidays

Planning a motorhome holiday to Europe with your dog? If your dog has been microchipped, has the relevant vaccinations, and a valid Animal Health Certificate then it should be no problem to travel to Europe. Other rules may apply so it is always worth checking in advance and doing your own research on the countries you want to explore.

**We look forward to welcoming you and your furry friend at our UK & Ireland depots.**

## Long Travel Discount FAQs

Can I use the long travel discount on my motorhome holiday to Europe?

Yes. If you are travelling to Europe for 15 nights or more, our long travel discount will automatically apply, helping you to save on the overall rental cost of your Europe motorhome holiday.

I want to spend a long motorhome holiday in Ireland, where should I travel?

Ireland offers a mix of stunning landscapes, rich history, and vibrant culture. Home to one of the world’s most famous driving routes, the Wild Atlantic Way is the perfect road trip for a long-haul motorhome holiday stretching over 2,500 km along the west coast of Ireland. Key destinations to visit include Cliffs of Moher, Ring of Kerry, Connemara, Newgrange, Glendalough, and Rock of Cashel.

I want to spend a long motorhome holiday in Scotland, where should I travel?

To explore all the beauty that Scotland has to offer, a long-haul motorhome holiday is needed to take you to the top hotspots and hidden gems. Top recommendations for your Scotland motorhome holiday include the famous North Coast 500 driving route taking you through the rugged coastlines, charming villages, and iconic landmarks including John O’Groats. Venturing beyond the North Coast 500, the Isle of Skye is a top destination offering dramatic landscapes and an adventure playground for hiking and outdoor pursuits. Popular Scotland driving routes also include Loch Lomond and the Trossachs National Park, The Outer Hebrides, The Argyll Coastal Route, Cairngorms National Park and the Southwest Coastal 300.

[Reach out to the team today](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/contact.html) for a free Scotland itinerary consultation.

I want to spend a long motorhome holiday in England, where should I travel?

To truly explore everything England has to offer, a longer motorhome holiday is needed. From the stunning lakes and mountains in The Lake District to the beautiful beaches in Cornwall. England’s beauty is undeniable, and your motorhome holiday can take you to the hidden gems and coastlines escapes. Other destinations to explore include the Peak District, Devon, The Yorkshire Dales, Norfolk Broads, The South Downs, and Northumberland.

[Reach out to the team today](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/contact.html) for a free England itinerary consultation.

I want to spend a long motorhome holiday in Northern Ireland where should I travel?

Northern Ireland might be small, but the country is packed with beautiful destinations and unmissable gems. Spending more time in Northern Ireland allows you to get off the beaten track and discover hidden treasures. One of the key driving adventures in Northern Ireland is the Causeway Coastal Route that includes iconic sights such as the Giant’s Causeway, Carrick-a-rede Rope Bridge, and Dunluce Castle. Beyond the Giant’s Causeway, Northern Ireland has an abundance of hidden gems including Mourne Mountains, Fermanagh Lakelands, Antrim Coast, Strangford Lough, Ards Peninsula, and Glenariff Forest Park.

[Reach out to the team today](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/contact.html) for a free Northern Ireland itinerary consultation.

Stay Longer, Pay Less Terms & Conditions

- Long hire discount applies to rentals 15 nights or more.
- Discount applies to nightly vehicle rate only.
- Subject to availability at all times based on fleet capacity assigned this special.
- Normal terms & conditions, payment conditions and cancellation fees apply.
- Only applies to NEW bookings.
- Rates are in currency of the pick-up depot.

How do I reserve my place on a Relocation Deal?

What is the cost of the extra nights?

If available, you can add extra nights at our standard nightly rate.

Relocation Terms and Conditions:

- Min nights: 3 nights.
- Additional nights can be added at the standard rate.
- Subject to availability at all times based on fleet capacity assigned this special.
- The Long-hire Discount does apply.
- This offer cannot be used in conjunction with any other special offers.
- Normal terms & conditions, payment conditions and cancellation fees apply.
- Only applies to NEW bookings.
- Rates are in currency of the pick-up branch.

Why are the Relocations priced so low?

Due to the last-minute nature of our Relocation deals it is only fair to price them starting at £5 per night.

I have never hired a motorhome before. Is a Relocation deal a good place to start?

Starting your motorhome hire experience with a Relocation deal is a great start. Not only is the rental cheaper, but it is also a generous amount of time for you to test the motorhome holiday experience.

What's included with the Relocation Deal?

Included in the deal you will get insurance for 1 driver aged 21-79 with a full driving licence (held for more than 2 years), kitchen kit and kettle, water hose, electric hook-up cable, unlimited mileage, and access to [campsite discounts.](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/promotions/campsite-guide.html)

## Hertz Price Match Policy
[skip to main content](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/promotions/price-match-policy.html#main)

# Just go Hertz **Price Match**

**The Just go Hertz Price Match ensures that we are always competitive on a Just go Hertz Motorhome wherever and whenever you shop on justgo.uk.com**

We are dedicated to offering the best possible value for our guests. If you find a cheaper Just go Hertz Motorhome for the same vehicle with the same travel dates on any other website, we will match the price.

![Just go Hertz Price Match](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/img/rear-of-motorhome.blurred.jpg)

## The **price match offer** applies provided that:

- The alternative price is for the same Just go Hertz motorhome.
- The collection and return dates are the same.
- The collection and return locations are the same.
- The alternative price is valid and available to book.
- The alternative price is displayed and charged in Great British Pounds (GBP) for UK hires or Euros (EURs) for Dublin hires.
- The alternative price is advised to Just go Hertz no later than 24 hours after confirming your booking with us.

To request a price match, just email **[enquiries@justgo.uk.com](mailto:enquiries@justgo.uk.com)** with the following information or click **‘Request Price Match’** below.

- the name of the website where the same vehicle is cheaper.
- a full screenshot or email of the cheaper offer (including the date and time visited).
- the cost of the cheaper vehicle (including currency in which it will be charged).
- your search criteria (dates/times/number of Guests, destination/mileage included etc).
- your name, email address and contact telephone number.
- Your Just go Hertz quote or booking reference (if you already have one).

Our reservations team, after verifying the information, will confirm if your price match has been successful within 24 hours.

[REQUEST PRICE MATCH](https://share.hsforms.com/1bZLWIQyhRt2uX8s1wfiMag3mgjv)

## **Price matches** for competitor quotes

Found a similar motorhome on a competitor website? Although our price match guarantee is designed to ensure you get your Just go Hertz motorhome at the best available price, if you have found a similar motorhome from a competitor, our team will try our best to apply our price match policy here too. It is not always possible, especially if it is not a comparable vehicle, or a model we don’t operate in our fleet but send the requested price match information and our motorhome experts will review your quote.

[REQUEST PRICE MATCH](https://share.hsforms.com/1bZLWIQyhRt2uX8s1wfiMag3mgjv)

![Just go Hertz Price Match](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/img/belfast-1.blurred.jpg)

## Price Match FAQs

Terms and Conditions

- The Just go Hertz price match will be based on the initial quote only. The price match is non-negotiable.
- Just go Hertz will validate the alternative price before matching.
- The price match relates to the daily vehicle hire rate only. Charges for optional extras or relating to other standard fees and charges (for example, credit card administration fees, location fees, and public holiday surcharges) are excluded from the price match offer.
- The price match guarantee may not apply during peak periods or when you use coupons/vouchers or any other promotional offers on the alternative website.
- Just go Hertz will offer one price match per booking.
- Just go Hertz reserves the right to refuse price match requests if quotes and/or vehicle is deemed to not be the same or vehicle is booked under different terms and conditions.
- Price matching based on currency exchange rates may or may not apply, this is at our discretion.
- Just go Hertz reserves the right to amend or withdraw this offer.

## Europe Motorhome **Road Trip Inspiration**

Whether you're heading to the sun-drenched coast of Spain, discovering the culture of Italy, or soaking in the history of Eastern Europe, our fleet of well-equipped motorhomes allows you to enjoy your Europe motorhome holiday on your own terms.

### European Motorhome Road Trip Ideas

- **Alpine Adventures in France and Switzerland:** Cross the Channel with your Just go Hertz motorhome and head towards the majestic French Alps. Explore the charming town of Chamonix, take in the view of Mont Blanc, and venture into Switzerland for more stunning mountain scenery. Then, continue your journey to Lake Garda in Italy for the ultimate gelato stop.
- **Coastal Cruising in Spain and France:** From the romantic castles of the Loire Valley to the golden beaches of Spain’s Costa Brava, you’ll have endless options for seaside exploration. Stop to enjoy the famous wines of Bordeaux, savour pintxos in the Basque Country, and soak up the sun along the French Riviera.
- **City and Culture in Eastern Europe:** For something different, head east to explore the cultural hubs of Prague and Vienna. Wander through centuries of history, take in architectural marvels, and savour the local cuisine—all without the restrictions of a traditional tour. Wind your way back via Germany, where you can toast to your trip in Bavaria.

![an image of the inside of a premium Justgo motorhome](https://www.hertzmotorhomehire.com/img/europe-inspiration-2.blurred.jpg)

Need help planning your route or figuring out the logistics? Our friendly team is here to assist you every step of the way. Whether you need advice on the best routes, ferry options, or campsites, we’ve got you covered. Reach out to us for all the support you need to make your European motorhome journey a success.

- **Ferry Discounts:** All Just go Hertz guests can enjoy access to fantastic savings on a wide range of ferry crossings when you book with the Caravan and Motorhome Club, enabling you to travel to Mainland Europe in your motorhome, for less. View some of the discounted routes here: [Visit the Caravan and Motorhome Club ferry offers page](https://www.caravanclub.co.uk/overseas-holidays/book-a-crossing/ferry-offers/).

Thanks to our partnership with the Caravan and Motorhome Club, you’ll have access to savings on a wide range of campsites, so you can find the perfect spot to park up for the night. Explore [European campsites available through the Club](https://www.caravanclub.co.uk/overseas-holidays/) and start planning your next great adventure.
- **Long Stay Discount:** Planning to stay on the open road for longer? # **Last Rays, Best Days**  15% OFF Motorhome Hire this September & October

Get ready for our new 15% off late summer motorhome hire offer. Hire any of our 2 – 6 berth motorhomes from any location across the UK & Ireland.

Whether it’s a spontaneous getaway or a long-awaited escape, this is your chance to enjoy the last rays and create your best days. Offering premium automatic motorhomes, equipped with everything you need for the perfect driving holiday, your next adventure starts and ends with Just go Hertz.

Save 15% when you book before 17 August 2025 for travel between 22 August 2025 – 31 October 2025. Ready for a motorhome adventure? Just go!

[Book Now](https://bookingprod.justgo.uk.com/?open-mobile=true&ac=HERTZ)

## Late Summer Motorhome Hire Offer

**Travel:** 22 August 2025 - 31 October 2025

**Book:** 14 July 2025 - 17 August 2025

### How to book

Book your late Summer motorhome adventure online using the booking engine on our website, contact our team by email [enquiries@justgo.uk.com](mailto:enquiries@justgo.uk.com) or call us on [0800 059 0905](tel:08000590905) (Monday-Saturday 9AM-5:30PM).

## **Superior as standard** means a well-equipped motorhome, which includes:

- ### On board bathrooms



Toilet and hot water shower as standard in all models

- ### Full kitchen



With fridge/freezer, gas hob, oven, kettle and toaster

- ### Cooking utensils



Including Crockery, Cutlery, Pots, Pans & cooking implements

- ### Personalised tour



Full vehicle familiarisation tour before you depart

- ### Comprehensive insurance



For 1 driver aged 21-79 years old

- ### Driver conveniences



Automatic transmission, cruise control and reversing camera

- ### Unlimited mileage



Across the UK & Ireland

- ### Campsite partnerships



With access to discounts on pitches across the UK

- ### 24/7 Road Assistance



Plus access to our dedicated On the Road Support team

- ### Gas bottle



To power your heating and keep your motorhome cosy all year round

- ### Essentials



Motorhome essentials included, such as a dustpan, hose and electric hook up

## Frequently Asked Questions

Find answers to commonly asked questions about motorhome hire

How much does it cost to hire a motorhome?

The cost of hiring a motorhome depends on the size of the vehicle and the time of year. For small and medium-sized motorhomes, you can expect to pay between £600 and £900 per week. Larger motorhomes with more features and space can cost up to £1500 for a weeklong hire. These prices are based on average rates. It's important to note that the most popular times for campervan hire are typically late spring, summer, and early autumn. During these times, you will find that prices are higher, and availability is limited. If you're looking to secure the best deal, it's a good idea to book your motorhome hire well in advance. Additionally, consider hiring outside of the peak season for lower prices and more availability.

Who are the best motorhome hire company?

Several reputable companies offer motorhome and campervan hire in the UK, each with its own strengths. Just go, Bunk Campers, Apollo and Hertz are popular choices with large fleets and availability. These all have locations across the UK and Ireland, giving access to Europe too.

Can you drive a motorhome on a standard UK driving licence?

Yes, you can drive a motorhome on a standard UK car driving license (Category B) if the motorhome’s Maximum Authorised Mass (MAM) is 3,500kg or less. All motorhomes on our fleet are under 3,500kg and can therefore be driven on a standard UK car driving license or equivalent.

What’s the difference between RV, Campervan and Motorhome hire?

In summary, campervans, RVs and motorhomes are self-drive vehicles designed for recreational travel and living, but they differ primarily in size and features. All three are used to describe motorhomes in the UK and Ireland, however the word campervan sometimes refers to the smaller, more compact VW. RVs (Recreational Vehicles) is a broader term, originally American that’s used to describe both motorhomes and campervans, but sometimes refers to larger, more luxurious vehicles with extensive amenities. Motorhomes are generally interpreted as being larger than campervans, offering more space and features like bathrooms, separate living and sleeping areas, with more extensive kitchens.

## Motorhomes grow in popularity

According to Bunk Campers, Industry figures show new motorhome registrations in the UK rose from 8487 in 2004 to 16447 in 2024 a 93.7% increase. [(4)](https://bunkcampers.com/#citation-1 "Bunk Campers - Motorhomes grow in popularity")

## Growing Popularity

Motorhome hire has seen a significant increase in popularity in recent years. According to a report by the UK's Camping and Caravanning Club, motorhome holidays have become increasingly popular with a 20% increase in Brits considering a touring holiday. Ownership is expected to grown by 40% by 2030. [(1)](https://www.caravanclub.co.uk/media-centre/press-releases/touring-holidays-surge-in-popularity-as-many-look-for-affordable-holiday-options/ "Camping and Caravanning Club - Touring Holidays Surge in Popularity as Many Look for Affordable Holiday Options")

## Environmental benefits

Motorhomes can be an eco-friendly way to travel compared to traditional holidays. A 500 mile road trip creates 20% of the carbon compared to a one-way flight from London to Tenerife (This has been calculated using carbon-clear.com and resurgence.org). Or if comparing to return flights it's just 10%. [(2)](https://bunkcampers.com/campervan-hire-and-sustainable-tourism/ "Bunk Campers - Campervan Hire and Sustainable Tourism")

## Economic impact

The broader Holiday Park and Campsite sector contributes significantly to the UK economy. According to a report by the UK Caravan & Camping Alliance (UKCCA), the industry generated £12.2 billion for the UK economy in visitor spending during 2024. [(3)](https://www.ukcca.org.uk/media/twjpssgp/ukcca-economic-impact-report-2024.pdf "UK Caravan Council - The Economic Impact of Caravanning and Motorhoming in the UK")

# Navigator - 4 berth With rear U-shaped lounge

This Navigator is an ideal family motorhome offering space, comfort and flexibility.

The best feature of this motorhome is the spacious rear lounge which means this 4 berth offers so much more space and flexibility and with 2 double beds on board, it is a great choice. It has a fully equipped kitchen and bathroom with toilet and shower. It comes complete with a 100 watt solar panel, great TV, roof mounted aerial and a full colour reversing camera.
